LANDMINES NEW ON THE 5TH: killing a `next dev` mid-write leaves a truncated `.next/dev/types/validator.ts` that fails the next `next build` type check (“Cannot find name”): clear `.next/dev/types` before a build. This harness’s bash heredocs collapse a doubled backslash to one (a regex class of backslash-or-slash lands as slash only): write test assertions with `path.join` rather than regexes carrying backslashes. SCHEDULE.md is staged with `git -c core.autocrlf=false add` after any rebuild from main’s bytes; docs/HANDOFF.md is CRLF on main and stays so through plain adds. The full suite times out on PageDesigner.test.tsx at 5 s whenever anything else runs: re-run the file alone, never raise the timeout. A reviewer reads files from git refs (`git show <branch>:<path>`), never from the working tree, when another branch may be checked out meanwhile.

Browser run recipe: the dev server is `PADDOCK_ENV=production npm run dev` launched with SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY set to .env.local's SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY value (never edit the file); the local Supabase (Docker) must be up; clear .next/dev/types and .next/types after switching branches or a cf:build; stop the dev server (the PID from netstat on :3000, taskkill //PID) before cf:build; the Playwright browser is signed in as the local admin and follows Formula 1 alone; set the viewport before every screenshot run (it drifted to 500 px once on the 1st; clips with scale 'css'); Playwright's MCP writes only under .playwright-mcp; the testing Worker is `wrangler deploy -c wrangler.testing.jsonc` then `npm run cf:populate:testing` (17 minutes; the dynamic pages measure before it finishes, the prerendered ones after); sizes by curl with a browser user agent for the HTML’s script count (the beacon is injected for browsers only), plain and --compressed for the bytes.

Landmines: the ones of the session-60 and 61 blocks hold (a route without generateStaticParams renders dynamically; the App Router ships every server-rendered element twice; the deploy-then-populate window; three merges in minutes deploy once; the Management API answers the last statement’s rows; String.prototype.replace reads $-patterns; the Bash tool’s double-quoted node -e strings lose a level of backslashes and MSYS rewrites a leading slash in an argument into a Git path (MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1); the registry test holds the seed migrations; the testing Worker reads prod’s design rows; a 404 under the catch-all is cached for the window); new on the 1st: the permission rail refuses the agent’s `git rm` even with the plan’s word (the operator runs it with the `!` prefix); after a deploy, cached prefetch payloads of other routes name chunk files the new Worker no longer ships (404 until their windows end, up to 3600 s), so a browser count right after a deploy is noisy; the Vercel project is paused (402 on every old deployment), so an old version is photographed by a throwaway git worktree of its commit (npm ci, bundle-content, next dev on another port), removed after; Turbopack exposes no option that merges client chunks (the count is the bundler’s), and the nomodule polyfill is always emitted; Seobility’s edge beacon count applies only to browser user agents.

Agenda for session 62: 1) B2 finished (merged or opened and merged on the standing word), prod measured, the baselines, B2 DONE, the records; 2) X9’s idle count on prod (the experiment is done); 3) the AdSense reading redone under the current login (the old page is unreachable; the records hold only S1 index the writing and S2 finish what shows); 4) the AdSense decisions, then S1 and S2; 5) P2.13 and the Phase 2 order; 6) the rest of X1–X8 after Phase 2; 7) the two Bing slots; 8) R16 after all; 9) O1 before 2026-12-31; 10) A4 on or after 2026-10-25.
```

### 🔵 The learning track — the start prompt for a LEARNING session (copy it whole, in the worktree)

Open a terminal in `C:\Dev\Personal\Motorsport-learn` (the worktree on branch `learn`, created 2026-10-01 with its dependencies installed) and start Claude Code there with this prompt:

```
Learning session L1 start. This is the LEARNING TRACK, not the programme: you are the teacher, the operator is the student and the owner of Paddock, and the aim is that they come to know and maintain every part of this repository themselves (their words of 2026-10-01: “I want the reigns on this project… I need a safe way to understand and know every nook and cranny… I have the capacity in my head to know it all and maintain it”). Read first: CLAUDE.md (every rule applies here too: plain words, no agents building, every subagent on Sonnet, no push to main, never weaken a check), the memory file project-paddock-learning-track, docs/HANDOFF.md’s learning block and the ledger’s change line of 2026-10-01 on the learning track. This worktree is on branch learn: run `git fetch origin && git merge origin/main` first (and `npm ci` if package-lock.json changed), so the code read is the current main. Never build a programme slot here; never touch the programme’s branches or the build session’s files; every exercise change goes on a branch learn/<part>-<exercise>, is never pushed or merged, and is reverted when the exercise ends (git checkout of the changed file is the exercise’s own undo, named before it is done).

The method: fourteen parts in dependency order (the table in docs/learning/README.md once it exists; until then the one in the ledger’s learning line and below), one at a time, at the operator’s pace, across as many sessions as it takes. For each part: a chapter in plain words (docs/learning/NN-<part>.md, also published as an artifact) with a tour of its files, every function named and explained, every term expanded; then the Socratic round: ask before telling, one question at a time, probe every gap, let the operator ask anything. A part is theirs when they have done four things on the real code, each recorded in docs/learning/progress.md: EXPLAIN IT BACK (they tell you what happens, e.g. when a visitor opens /calendar, you probe); PREDICT THE TEST (you show a test’s name and setup, they say what it asserts, you run it together: npx vitest run <file>); FIND IT (you name a symptom, they name the file and line); BREAK IT ON PURPOSE (they change one line on a learn branch, predict which test goes red, run it, put it back).

The parts (code lines, tests): 1 the request path (middleware.ts, app/(app)/layout.tsx, worker.ts, open-next.config.ts, next.config.ts, wrangler.jsonc; ~1,500) · 2 the content model (content/series/*, lib/series.ts, lib/types.ts; ~2,500 + 23,000 of content the operator already owns) · 3 the schedule (the ICS feeds, lib/calendar-grid.ts, lib/weekend.ts, components/calendar; ~4,000) · 4 results and standings (lib/results: overrides.ts and session-classification.ts first, then the fifteen parsers, then openf1.ts and fom-api.ts; 6,900 + 4,700 tests) · 5 the live data store (lib/kv.ts, lib/source-snapshot.ts, lib/f1-cache.ts, scripts/warm-live-data.mts, app/api/cron; ~5,000) · 6 the pages still in code (app/(app) routes; 12,200) · 7 the shared components (components: the chrome AppShell/BottomBar/Footer/CookieConsent, the series pieces, the home HomeLead, the blog, components/f1, components/weekend; 29,000; split again when reached) · 8 the designer’s model (lib/design; 12,100 + 9,700 tests) · 9 the designer’s screens (components/designer; 15,400) · 10 the APIs (app/api; 8,100) · 11 accounts (lib/auth; 1,000) · 12 the extras (lib/betting, lib/information, lib/upgrades, SEO and JSON-LD, weather, push; ~7,000) · 13 scripts and operations (scripts/, .claude/hooks, docs/plan/render-ledger.mjs, the deploy; 5,300) · 14 the tests as a craft (43,500).

The first session, on the operator’s word for the three new files (docs/learning/README.md the method and the parts; docs/learning/progress.md the record; docs/learning/00-the-map.md the map): write the map (one page: every part, its files and sizes, what controls it today — code, the designer, a content file, a setting — and which kind of control it should end up under: the designer, a switch, or code with a guide), publish it as an artifact, and let the operator mark where to begin; then part 1 as the first chapter and the first Socratic round. The first FIND IT exercise is real: the home’s This weekend boxes show session starts in UTC (components/HomeLead.tsx:123, timeLabel; the fix is the LocalTime piece as components/SessionDayNote does); the operator finds and fixes it on a learn branch with you beside them; the production fix is R17 in the build session, so the learn branch is not merged. Keep every message short and plain; expand every term every time; never narrate code at the operator; ask. At the session’s end: progress.md updated on the operator’s word, a one-paragraph note of where to resume added to the top of docs/learning/README.md, the learn branch committed locally (never pushed unless the operator asks).
```

Landmines: the ones of the session-60 block hold (a route without generateStaticParams renders dynamically; the manifest is the proof; the testing Worker reads prod's design rows; the first render on a fresh Worker is slow; a 404 under the catch-all is cached for the window; the Management API's query answers the last statement's rows; String.prototype.replace reads $-patterns, build records by split-and-join; the Bash tool's double-quoted node -e strings lose a level of backslashes, write scripts to files; the page-registry test holds the seed migrations to the registry, corrections by a *_pages_seed_*.sql the test reads as the last word); new this night: the App Router ships every server-rendered element twice (the markup and the inline flight payload), so a page weighs about twice its markup and folds hide but do not lighten; a page.tsx exports only the fields Next knows (server/typescript/rules/config.js), shared markup lives in a component module; the sitemap is regenerated on the Worker every six hours (initialRevalidateSeconds 21600), so what it lists must come from the content bundle, never from RELEASES.md (RELEASE_INDEX); cf:build runs `npm run build`, whose prebuild regenerates the content bundle; the deploy:testing order (deploy, then populate) leaves a window where a prerendered page renders on the Worker without its file; NASCAR, IndyCar and WRC name their race sessions by event, so isRaceLikeTitle finds no race for them and their session pages show no classification (DTM's neither: no per-race source); the results feeds' GT World races carry no dates; the calendar's ICS uids are shorter than assumed, so the payload saving was a fifth, not a third; the PSI API's anonymous daily quota is small.

Agenda for session 61: 1) the three X6 PRs on the words, prod measured, the baselines, X6 DONE, the records, the board; 2) the B2 question answered and, on the word, its plan; 3) X9 in plan mode with a critic; 4) the AdSense decisions on the word, then S1 and S2 as slots; 5) P2.13 and the Phase 2 order; 6) the rest of X1–X8 after Phase 2; 7) the two Bing slots after Phase 2; 8) O1 before 2026-12-31; 9) A4 on or after 2026-10-25; 10) D1 last.
```

Landmines learned this session: the frame hands a route's address parts to the components since P2.17 (page-frame.tsx routeParams), the conditions keep P2.6's rule; a 'use client' file's exports must not be imported by the client-safe parser (ICON_NAMES moved to lib/design/destinations.ts); lib/weather.ts's tile builders take the label as a parameter so the file stays a leaf; by name alone the circuit matcher returns the wrong track for a relocated round (the Bahrain Grand Prix → Sakhir): always venueCandidates with the curated venue first; jsdom lacks matchMedia and scrollIntoView (stub both in a client test); react-hooks/set-state-in-effect is at error (copy WeekendTabs' disable block for a post-mount setState); renderToStaticMarkup omits the <!-- --> text-node comments SSR inserts, so a grep of the live page must not assume contiguous text; the records scripts check every anchor before writing and a replaceLine prefix must be unique across the handoff's older blocks; the artifact tool cannot update an artifact published under another login (publish anew, record the link); Seobility's crawl started as R15 was deploying, so it may be walking the filter chips' combinations; a background poll dies with the session (re-check prod by hand after a restart). P2.12: a var() cannot fill Leaflet’s presentation attributes (a marker without a colour takes the accent through a class in globals.css); react-leaflet follows a changed tile url but not a changed attribution; the Property Editor’s grouped choices are pill groups, not selects (a scripted probe reads the text, not the selects); a records script whose append() re-reads the file overwrites its own earlier edit (plan every edit, write once); Playwright’s MCP writes only under .playwright-mcp; the Read tool renders no PDF here (pdftotext -layout in /mingw64/bin does). P2.15: the Madring fix of 1.0.97 is an alias in content/circuits.json (“Spanish Grand Prix (Madrid)” outweighing Barcelona’s under the longest-alias rule), not a curated round venue; only 21 of the 98 circuits share their guide’s slug (Baku’s guide is baku-city-circuit), so the bridge getTrackInfoByCircuitSlug comes before getInfoEntry; dependingOn works on a boolean parent (String(true) === 'true'); the Property Editor’s grouped choices and switches are pill groups (Yes/No), not selects; the local dev server dies when a build chain kills its PID (expected) and a records script’s regex anchors must match the exact Active line.
Agenda for session 60: 1) X7 built on the approved plan, its PR, the merge on the word, the records; 2) X6 in plan mode, then X9; 3) the AdSense decisions on the word, then S1 (index the writing) and S2 (finish what shows) as slots, Search Console read first; 4) P2.13 and the Phase 2 order; 5) the rest of X1–X8 after Phase 2; 6) after Phase 2 the two Bing slots, research first; 7) O1 before 2026-12-31; 8) P2.21 at its turn; 9) A4 on or after 2026-10-25; 10) D1 last.
```

### The approved X7 plan (2026-09-30, ~18:35Z; the critic’s fixes folded; build it in session 60)

# X7 — the edge cache for the session pages and the designer-made pages — the plan

Reading and planning pass only; no code changed. A fresh-context Sonnet plan critic (312,315 tokens, 103 reads: the installed Next 16 docs, Next's build source, OpenNext's interceptor, this checkout's prerender manifest) found the cause the code did not show and two blocking fixes, all folded below: **the catch-all has no `generateStaticParams`, so Next renders it dynamically whatever the page does** (`generate-static-params.md:302`: "You must always return an array from generateStaticParams, even if it's empty. Otherwise, the route will be dynamically rendered"; `.next/prerender-manifest.json` lists the drivers and weekend routes under `dynamicRoutes` and neither the catch-all nor the session route anywhere; OpenNext's `cacheInterceptor.js:214-215` keys on exactly those fields); the registry's `rendering` for the session route (`lib/design/page-registry.ts:83`) must flip to `cached` with the route or `page-registry.test.ts:54-58` fails; the WEC POST is reachable on an unseeded snapshot key; a single-page publish does not revalidate the `/__view` variants; the `dynamic = 'error'` bisect would have passed clean and proven nothing. The operator's order of 2026-09-30 (~10:00Z): "Once p2.15 is done then do X7 first (cache the session and catch-all pages), then X6 (page weight), then the JS count"; the Seobility export is the most important report the site has had and no finding is dismissed as design. X7 is the slot the triage named (https://claude.ai/code/artifact/4c2f6e9c-8fee-407d-accb-40c1cbdb90f5); it enters the ledger by a dated line when it starts, with X6 and the JavaScript count (X9) planned beside it in a new phase X. Read in the main agent (rule 7); every claim below read at file:line or measured on prod this evening.

## Context

**What is slow, measured on prod 2026-09-30 ~17:20Z (`curl -D -`, time to first byte from Greece):** every session page answers `Cache-Control: private, no-cache, no-store` and renders per visitor: `/series/f1/weekend/15/qualifying` 2.5 s (the OpenF1 analyses computed for each visitor), `/series/wec/weekend/6/6-hours-of-fuji-race` 1.6 s, `/series/dtm/weekend/6/race-1` 0.43 s. Every designer-made page answers the same header: `/calendar` 2.1 s, `/calendar?s=motogp` 2.1 s, `/news` 0.77 s, and even a plain row page with no family, `/history/monza`, 0.21 s but uncached. The ISR pages answer `s-maxage` with `x-opennext-cache: HIT` at 0.2–0.35 s: the home, `/drivers/<slug>`, `/information/**`, `/series/<slug>/weekend/<round>`. Seobility counted 1,813 pages at 0.5–1 s from Germany and hit 88 timeouts and 500s during its crawl; those are these pages.

**The cache stack exists** (`open-next.config.ts`): the R2 incremental cache behind a long-lived regional cache, the Durable Object queue that stale pages need for background revalidation, the sharded tag cache that makes `revalidatePath` work (proven 2026-08-03), and since R13 C2 `enableCacheInterception: true`: a route the prerender manifest knows is answered from the cache by the routing layer before Next's server starts. The ISR model in the repo is one shape: `export const revalidate = N` and `export function generateStaticParams() { return []; }` (`app/(app)/drivers/[slug]/page.tsx:24-28` at 3600; `app/(app)/series/[slug]/weekend/[round]/page.tsx:45-50` at 300; `app/(app)/information/[topic]/[slug]/page.tsx:16-21` at 3600).

**The session route opts out by choice:** `app/(app)/series/[slug]/weekend/[round]/[session]/page.tsx:92` `export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`, with a comment (`:70-91`) that records the reasoning: the account gate that justified it is gone, `revalidate = 60` "still built as ƒ (dynamic)", `fetchCache = 'default-cache'` "still ƒ", so "at least one fetch in this page's fan-out passes no-store". Two facts against that reading: the build summary marks every route without prerendered params `ƒ`, the cached drivers and weekends included; and the only explicit `cache: 'no-store'` fetch in the data layer is the POST in `lib/results/wec.ts:272` (the FIA WEC results form), which the weekend page's fan-out reaches too (`fetchClassClassifications`) while the weekend page is cached on prod, and which the Worker never runs under `DATA_SOURCE=db` (the snapshot tier answers). Every other upstream call uses `next: { revalidate }` (`lib/results/*.ts`, `lib/standings/*.ts`, `lib/openf1/client.ts:103`, `lib/ics.ts:73`, `lib/news.ts:237`, `lib/weather.ts:94`). The session page itself calls no request API (no `cookies()`, `headers()`, `searchParams`, `connection()`, `noStore`: grep). Its classifications are KV-persisted once a session ends (`:94-96`), so a cached render loses nothing after the flag.

**The designer-made pages opt out somewhere the code does not show:** `app/(app)/[...catchall]/page.tsx:47` `export const revalidate = 300`; `connection()` only for a row with `rendering: 'dynamic'` (`:98`); `currentVisitor()` (the session cookie, `lib/auth/server.ts:49`) only when a scheme other than public or a visitor condition is asked (`schemesAsked` at `lib/design/page-document.ts:906-911` excludes `public`; `lib/design/authz-evaluate.ts:10-14` says so). The loaders (`lib/design/live-page.ts`, `resolve-page.ts`, `pages.ts`, `lists.ts`, `shortcuts.ts`, `build-options.ts`, `appearance.ts`, `authz.ts`) read Supabase through `betDb()` (`lib/betting/client.ts:22`, supabase-js with no cache option) and memoise with React `cache()`; the middleware rewrites a stateful address to `/__view/<state>/<path>` (`middleware.ts:136-144`), a distinct cached path by design. The R13 C2 Inbox line already says it: "something in that render calls a dynamic API or a no-store fetch. Run the Debug trace on the local server, name the call." The catch-all's test (`app/(app)/[...catchall]/page.test.tsx:25-27, :198-225`) mocks `connection` and holds that a cached page never calls it, so the opt-out is below the route: a loader, a component's read, or the middleware's forwarded headers. Naming it is the first step of the build, not a guess in the plan.

**Revalidation on publish exists:** `app/api/admin/design/pages/[id]/revisions/route.ts:116-117` calls `revalidatePath(detail.page.path)` (or `'page'` for a pattern) when a revision is published; the shared components' routes call `revalidatePath('/', 'layout')` (appearance, application, authz, lists, `build-options/[key]` at `:76` for the weekend route). The tag cache's regional TTL is 5 s, so a publish shows within seconds.

## Decision scan

**Fixed by:** the operator's order and words; the ISR model of the drivers, weekends and information pages; the OpenNext cache stack and the interception of manifest routes; the catch-all's `rendering` row flag and its authz rule (a page asking for a scheme stays per request); the publish revalidation; rule 6 (the numbers before and after, on the testing Worker first as R13 C2 did, then prod); `docs/perf-baselines.md`'s protocol (a dated section appended, never overwritten).

**Defaults I take:**
- **The session pages become ISR like the weekend page:** `export const revalidate = 300` and `export function generateStaticParams() { return []; }` replace `export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`; the comment block at `:70-91` is rewritten to the measured truth (the `ƒ` misread; the one `no-store` POST the Worker never runs). No per-request exception for a running session: the home already lives with the same five-minute window for its live states, the loader writes results every twenty minutes, and the client pieces (the countdown, the local times) tick regardless; a running session's page is re-rendered at most every five minutes on demand instead of for every visitor, which is when it is most visited. The 8 s upstream timeout, the KV persistence and the Suspense streaming stay.
- **The designer-made pages:** the cause is the missing export, not a hidden call (the critic's finding: no request API is reachable on a wholly public row; the catch-all's 14 tests pass because they never touch the prerender manifest). The fix is the model's shape: `export async function generateStaticParams() { return []; }` beside `revalidate = 300` in `app/(app)/[...catchall]/page.tsx`; the `rendering: 'dynamic'` rows keep `connection()`, a page asking for a scheme keeps reading the visitor, exactly as today. The proof is not a thrown error but the manifest: after the build, `.next/prerender-manifest.json` lists `/[...catchall]` and the session route under `dynamicRoutes` as it lists `/drivers/[slug]`; then the testing Worker's headers.
- **The session route's registry row** (`lib/design/page-registry.ts:83`, `P('/series/[slug]/weekend/[round]/[session]', 'Session', 'series', 'dynamic', true)`) flips to `'cached'` in the same commit: `page-registry.test.ts:54-58` reads each route file's source and holds the registry to it.
- **Known and accepted, recorded in the PR:** a single-page publish calls `revalidatePath(page.path)` (`revisions/route.ts:116-117`), which does not reach that page's `/__view/<state>/<path>` variants (a reader's sort or filter state); once the catch-all caches, a variant can stay up to 300 s stale after a publish. Invisible today because nothing caches. The default is to accept it (a transient state, five minutes) and record it; the alternative, `revalidatePath('/[...catchall]', 'page')` on publish, purges every designer-made page and is offered as a question. The `lib/results/wec.ts:272` POST stays: the Worker does not run it while a `results:wec` snapshot is stored, but an unseeded key falls through to it (`lib/source-snapshot.ts:189-195`), so that one WEC race-session path renders dynamically until the loader seeds the key; called out, not changed.
- **Measured before it ships:** the testing Worker (`npm run deploy:testing`) answers the six pages above with `s-maxage` and, on the second request, `x-opennext-cache: HIT`; the time to first byte quoted before and after; then prod after the merge; a dated section appended to `docs/perf-baselines.md`.
- **Readers see:** the same pages, faster (0.2–0.35 s to the first byte instead of 0.4–2.5 s); a session's result may appear up to five minutes later than today; a designer publish still shows within seconds.
- **The ledger:** X7 starts with a dated line on the word (phase X "Seobility", with X6 page weight and X9 the JavaScript count planned beside it; the rest of X1–X8 stay drafted in the triage until their word).
- **New file, on the plan's approval** (the law: name, format, purpose): `app/(app)/series/[slug]/weekend/[round]/[session]/page.test.tsx` (TSX, vitest, node): reads the route file's source and holds `export const revalidate = 300`, `generateStaticParams`, and the absence of `force-dynamic`; the catch-all's fix gets its case in the existing `app/(app)/[...catchall]/page.test.tsx` (a wholly public row page renders without the named call; a `rendering: 'dynamic'` row still calls `connection()`; a page asking a scheme still reads the visitor).

**Needs your word:** none beyond the plan's approval. Three defaults to object to if you want them otherwise: the five-minute window for sessions (the weekend page's); no per-request exception for a running session (a live page five minutes stale at most); the `/__view` variants accepted up to five minutes stale after a single-page publish (the alternative purges every designer-made page on each publish).

## The build

1. **Tests first, red:** the new route-scan test on the session page (red while `force-dynamic` stands and `generateStaticParams` is missing); the same scan on the catch-all (`generateStaticParams` present, `revalidate = 300` kept) in its existing test file; `lib/design/page-registry.test.ts` red once the session route changes until its row flips.
2. **The catch-all:** `export async function generateStaticParams() { return []; }` beside `revalidate`; nothing else in its render path changes.
3. **The session route:** `force-dynamic` out, `revalidate = 300` and `generateStaticParams` in, the comment at `:70-91` rewritten to the measured truth (the `ƒ` misread; the missing export; the one no-store POST behind a stored snapshot); `lib/design/page-registry.ts:83` to `'cached'`.
4. **The manifest check:** after `DATA_SOURCE=db npm run cf:build`, `.next/prerender-manifest.json` lists both routes under `dynamicRoutes` (the drivers' shape); quoted in the PR.
5. **Gates:** tsc 0, lint 0 errors (the two known), vitest full; `DATA_SOURCE=db npm run cf:build` and the dry run quoted.
6. **The testing Worker:** `npm run deploy:testing`; `curl -D -` twice on `/series/f1/weekend/15/qualifying`, `/series/wec/weekend/6/6-hours-of-fuji-race`, `/series/dtm/weekend/6/race-1`, `/calendar`, `/news`, `/history/monza`: `s-maxage` on the first, `x-opennext-cache: HIT` on the second, the time to first byte before and after in a table; a designer publish on the testing host shows within seconds (the tag cache).
7. **The reviewer, the trio, the PR, the merge on the word; then prod measured the same way; `docs/perf-baselines.md` appended.**

Records: the ledger (phase X opened with X7 started, X6 and X9 planned, the triage's text; two dated lines: the operator's order of the 30th, X7's start), CHANGELOG 1.0.223, RELEASES ("the race pages and the calendar answer from the edge cache; a result may show up to five minutes later"), package.json, docs/pull-requests.md, the schedule.

## Pre-mortem

- **A stale page after a deploy** points at chunk hashes that no longer exist for the first visitor of each cached page (the HANDOFF's finding of session 35). Nothing new: every ISR page has it today; noted, not this slot's.
- **A result five minutes late** on a running session's page: the home already accepts it; the classification comes from the loader every twenty minutes; if the operator wants live pages per request, `connection()` under `weekendIsLive` is a one-line addition, at the cost of the busiest pages staying slow.
- **Eighteen hundred pages revalidating:** each is rendered on its first visit after the deploy and every five minutes while visited; the OpenF1 analyses' cost moves from every visitor to one render per window.
- **The catch-all's call is a design choice** (personalisation on every page): the fix becomes a question with the two screens, not a silent change.
- **Search engines:** Seobility's 1,813 medium-response pages fall to whatever is visited within a window; the 88 crawl errors were the per-request renders under load.

## Won't touch

The loader and its schedule; `lib/results/wec.ts` and the other fetchers' policies; the middleware's rewrite and the view vocabulary; the catch-all's authz and `rendering` rules; any page's document; the OpenNext configuration (the interception, the caches) unless the named call requires it, and then as a question.

## Verification

The route-scan test red then green; the catch-all's cases; tsc 0; lint 0 errors; vitest full; cf:build and the dry run quoted; the testing Worker's headers and first-byte times before and after in a table on the review page; a fresh-context Sonnet reviewer before the merge; prod measured after the deploy; the perf baselines appended; the Seobility rerun is the operator's.

### Won’t touch until you say
Any slot beyond its decision scan; Home’s document (the five headings are yours); any prod design row (the four components’ placements); the cost PR; branch deletions; any prod Supabase write without “apply <id>”; any push to `main`; agents on Fable; agents building (rule 7); a new dependency; social sharing widgets.

The dev server MUST be `PADDOCK_ENV=production npm run dev` (in the background, its log in the scratchpad): without the flag the designer is read-only. The sign-in helper lives in the session's scratchpad and is gone with it: rewrite signin-redirect.cjs (a node http server on 127.0.0.1:4599; on GET /go it reads CLERK_SECRET_KEY from .env.local, POSTs https://api.clerk.com/v1/sign_in_tokens with { user_id: 'user_3J40T0N8dWLFMqgMb06DjsgIj3c', expires_in_seconds: 300 } and the bearer key, and answers 302 to http://localhost:3000/sign-in?__clerk_ticket=<token>; logs only "ticket issued"); start it in the background, `page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:4599/go')`, wait a few seconds, then open the designer (a page that stays on /sign-in still signs the session in). `page.setViewportSize({ width: 1500, height: 1000 })` first. The tiles are elements with aria-label "Component: Data region" (`page.locator('[aria-label="Component: Data region"]').nth(i)`); getByLabel and getByRole match substrings: `{ exact: true }` where names nest ("Save" against "Save and Run Page", "Series" against "Series slug", "Property Editor", "Gallery"); Save is disabled until the document differs from the stored one (a Heading set to the preset's own name changes the document and not the page). Monza's page id is 250f3a59-43e6-46b5-b1ef-d1dc7b899fef, Home's local id 7d85c16f-3de7-4cea-8718-5cd95bb25736; the designer opens at /admin/designer?page=<id> on the newest draft; the Region tab holds "Source type" and the source's parameters; the Attributes tab holds Preset, View, Rows, Heading, Pinned post, the Card and Actions groups; Save then GET /api/admin/design/pages/<id> for `newest.id` (or read the local page_revision table by psql: `docker exec supabase_db_Motorsport psql -U postgres -d postgres -At -c "…"`); the preview at /preview/<rev> and the Debug trace at GET /api/admin/design/debug?rev=<rev>&level=app answer only a row page or a page served from rows — Home has neither while its route file stands (P3.18); its check is the served http://localhost:3000/ (about 19 s cold locally: six seasons fetched live for the Latest result). Screenshots must be saved under .playwright-mcp/ (the tool's allowed root), are embedded by a node script into the review page (base64 data URIs) and the page is published with the Artifact tool. Before cf:build: stop the dev server and the helper by the ports' PIDs (`netstat -ano | grep ":3000 "`, `taskkill //PID <pid> //F //T`).
Landmines: concurrent dynamic imports of one module (a Promise.all of readers that each `import()` it) are several loads, and vitest's module runner hands the real module to every import but the first while the first is in flight — a fan-out loads its graphs in turn and reads together (source-read.ts resultsGraph); a fixed wait in a renderer test (60 ms for a beside-region) races under the full suite's load — poll with vi.waitFor; a test that names a component by its name (the Plug-ins editor's "What it changed") is missed by a grep on keys; the Table draws a right-aligned number column before a left-aligned one with no gap; a fixture date near today rots a countdown assertion (use 2030); a vi.mock factory may define a helper inside itself and export several functions from it; a bare arrow in a mock cannot be re-pointed — use vi.fn() (typed with an explicit generic when the default implementation takes no parameter); vitest 4's restoreAllMocks does not reset vi.fn implementations — reset them in beforeEach; React escapes the apostrophe as &#x27; in renderToStaticMarkup; `toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })` reads "Sunday 6 September"; Home's literals use the straight apostrophe; OpenNext logs "Failed to parse JSON file … .next/cache/fetch-cache/…" warnings for stale cache entries (harmless); the served page's flight <script> tags carry build-hashed chunk names and sit inside the streamed subtree (the parity script strips them first); the artifacts published under a previous login are gone after /login (the board is 87eea43d-b6f0-47fa-a1c4-b10dacb2ff01, P2.24's scan page 8db7e606-3546-498f-b9ff-154db39f0ac5, PR A's review page a4edd962-5678-40c3-8b06-048322339d20, B1's 435d574c-bfa9-4371-afca-2485e813744f, B2's bb8c0ea0-002d-4502-917e-52a6bec1cfbc, PR C's 1ce6f9ab-24e0-48a6-8f00-ea8219a62fde, the progress report 9b8b0a28-91e8-4fca-b08b-985ac612c267).
Agenda for session 57: 1) the prod apply of 20260923220000 on the word, rehearsed first (above); 2) P2.4's decision scan and plan (plan mode, a Sonnet critic, ExitPlanMode for the word), then tests first; 3) the operator's three actions after PR C, at their turn; 4) the Inbox: why prod's Calendar renders per request; the Saved Views editor's screenshot once the sign-in helper may run again (it was stopped by the system for memory — start it only when asked). This run's landmines: Next 16 hands a catch-all segment percent-encoded — never a percent-escaped state in a path, the variant segment is base64url; a build beside the dev server corrupts .next/dev/types — stop the server by its port PID, delete that folder, restart; React 19 reorders form and input attributes in its output (class before name, action before method) — assert with [^>]* between attributes; a Response's text() drops a leading byte-order mark — check the bytes; a heredoc over ~6 KB is cut by the Bash tool — write files in parts to the scratchpad and cat them; a heredoc appended to a CRLF file lands as LF — normalise a new or appended file to CRLF first; `git add -A -- <paths>` stages every change under the paths, so check `git status --short` for strays before the commit.

Rules: every subagent on Sonnet or Haiku, one at a time, writing to files (the general-purpose type; the Plan type cannot); no agent builds; the settings' force runs subagents on Sonnet and the hook checks it. This run's figures: the plan critic 399,562 (said ~250k), the reviewer 386,082 (said ~250k), the R8 reviewer 195,217 (said ~60k; a subagent's hand-back frame carries no figure, the task notification does — read both). A blocking finding is taken in a second commit with the test the reviewer names, and the gates re-run after it. Merge only on the word; `gh pr merge <n> --squash --delete-branch=false --subject "<title> (#<n>)"`; never push to main; the records PR merges under the same word. Every plan approval and every default taken is a dated line in the ledger's changes list the same day (UTC). The ledger is one-space JSON with CRLF (JSON.stringify(l, null, 1) with \n → \r\n and a trailing CRLF round-trips it), then `node docs/plan/render-ledger.mjs` and `npx vitest run lib/design/plan-ledger.test.ts`. Gates after the last edit: tsc 0 · lint 0 errors (the two known `_encoding` warnings) · full vitest · `node .claude/hooks/test.mjs` · `DATA_SOURCE=db npm run cf:build` in the FOREGROUND with the dev server and the helper stopped by their ports' PIDs, then `npx wrangler deploy --dry-run` with Total Upload quoted against 39409.56 KiB (alarm at 80% of 65536; the figure moves with the day's prerendered content). Tests first, seen red: write the tests before the implementation and run them (17 red across 10 files this run, plus a test file failing to load on a missing export); the red count is the evidence. The Bash tool's command input is cut somewhere above ~8 KB ("unexpected EOF while looking for matching quote"): keep a heredoc-carrying command under ~6 KB and write long edits to files first; edits to one file go in one call (parallel calls on one file race).
Browser run recipe: Docker Desktop and the local Supabase up first (`docker info`; `npx supabase start`; REST 200 on 127.0.0.1:54321). The dev server MUST be `PADDOCK_ENV=production npm run dev` (in the background, its log in the scratchpad): without the flag the designer is read-only. The sign-in helper lives in the session's scratchpad and is gone with it: rewrite signin-redirect.cjs (a node http server on 127.0.0.1:4599; on GET /go it reads CLERK_SECRET_KEY from .env.local, POSTs https://api.clerk.com/v1/sign_in_tokens with { user_id: 'user_3J40T0N8dWLFMqgMb06DjsgIj3c', expires_in_seconds: 300 } and the bearer key, and answers 302 to http://localhost:3000/sign-in?__clerk_ticket=<token>; logs only "ticket issued"); start it in the background, `page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:4599/go')`, wait a few seconds, then open the designer (a page that stays on /sign-in still signs the session in). `page.setViewportSize({ width: 1500, height: 1000 })` first. The tiles are elements with aria-label "Component: Data region" (`page.locator('[aria-label="Component: Data region"]').nth(i)`); getByLabel and getByRole match substrings: `{ exact: true }` where names nest ("Save" against "Save and Run Page", "Series" against "Series slug", "Property Editor", "Gallery"); Save is disabled until the document differs from the stored one (a Heading set to the preset's own name changes the document and not the page). Monza's page id is 250f3a59-43e6-46b5-b1ef-d1dc7b899fef, Home's local id 7d85c16f-3de7-4cea-8718-5cd95bb25736; the designer opens at /admin/designer?page=<id> on the newest draft; the Region tab holds "Source type" and the source's parameters; the Attributes tab holds Preset, View, Rows, Heading, Pinned post, the Card and Actions groups; Save then GET /api/admin/design/pages/<id> for `newest.id` (or read the local page_revision table by psql: `docker exec supabase_db_Motorsport psql -U postgres -d postgres -At -c "…"`); the preview at /preview/<rev> and the Debug trace at GET /api/admin/design/debug?rev=<rev>&level=app answer only a row page or a page served from rows — Home has neither while its route file stands (P3.18); its check is the served http://localhost:3000/ (about 19 s cold locally: six seasons fetched live for the Latest result). Screenshots must be saved under .playwright-mcp/ (the tool's allowed root), are embedded by a node script into the review page (base64 data URIs) and the page is published with the Artifact tool. Before cf:build: stop the dev server and the helper by the ports' PIDs (`netstat -ano | grep ":3000 "`, `taskkill //PID <pid> //F //T`).
Landmines: concurrent dynamic imports of one module (a Promise.all of readers that each `import()` it) are several loads, and vitest's module runner hands the real module to every import but the first while the first is in flight — a fan-out loads its graphs in turn and reads together (source-read.ts resultsGraph); a fixed wait in a renderer test (60 ms for a beside-region) races under the full suite's load — poll with vi.waitFor; a test that names a component by its name (the Plug-ins editor's "What it changed") is missed by a grep on keys; the Table draws a right-aligned number column before a left-aligned one with no gap; a fixture date near today rots a countdown assertion (use 2030); a vi.mock factory may define a helper inside itself and export several functions from it; a bare arrow in a mock cannot be re-pointed — use vi.fn() (typed with an explicit generic when the default implementation takes no parameter); vitest 4's restoreAllMocks does not reset vi.fn implementations — reset them in beforeEach; React escapes the apostrophe as &#x27; in renderToStaticMarkup; `toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })` reads "Sunday 6 September"; Home's literals use the straight apostrophe; OpenNext logs "Failed to parse JSON file … .next/cache/fetch-cache/…" warnings for stale cache entries (harmless); the served page's flight <script> tags carry build-hashed chunk names and sit inside the streamed subtree (the parity script strips them first); the artifacts published under a previous login are gone after /login (the board is 87eea43d-b6f0-47fa-a1c4-b10dacb2ff01, P2.24's scan page 8db7e606-3546-498f-b9ff-154db39f0ac5, PR A's review page a4edd962-5678-40c3-8b06-048322339d20, B1's 435d574c-bfa9-4371-afca-2485e813744f, B2's bb8c0ea0-002d-4502-917e-52a6bec1cfbc, PR C's 1ce6f9ab-24e0-48a6-8f00-ea8219a62fde, the progress report 9b8b0a28-91e8-4fca-b08b-985ac612c267).

Browser run recipe: Docker Desktop and the local Supabase up first (`docker info`; `npx supabase start`; REST 200 on 127.0.0.1:54321). The dev server MUST be `PADDOCK_ENV=production npm run dev` (in the background, its log in the scratchpad): without the flag the designer is read-only. Screenshots must be saved under .playwright-mcp/ (the tool's allowed root), are embedded by a node script into the review page (base64 data URIs) and the page is published with the Artifact tool. Before cf:build: stop the dev server and the helper by the ports' PIDs (`netstat -ano | grep ":3000 "`, `taskkill //PID <pid> //F //T`).
Landmines: concurrent dynamic imports of one module (a Promise.all of readers that each `import()` it) are several loads, and vitest's module runner hands the real module to every import but the first while the first is in flight — a fan-out loads its graphs in turn and reads together (source-read.ts resultsGraph); a fixed wait in a renderer test (60 ms for a beside-region) races under the full suite's load — poll with vi.waitFor; a test that names a component by its name (the Plug-ins editor's "What it changed") is missed by a grep on keys; the Table draws a right-aligned number column before a left-aligned one with no gap; a fixture date near today rots a countdown assertion (use 2030); a vi.mock factory may define a helper inside itself and export several functions from it; a bare arrow in a mock cannot be re-pointed — use vi.fn() (typed with an explicit generic when the default implementation takes no parameter); vitest 4's restoreAllMocks does not reset vi.fn implementations — reset them in beforeEach; React escapes the apostrophe as &#x27; in renderToStaticMarkup; `toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' })` reads "Sunday 6 September"; Home's literals use the straight apostrophe; OpenNext logs "Failed to parse JSON file … .next/cache/fetch-cache/…" warnings for stale cache entries (harmless); the served page's flight <script> tags carry build-hashed chunk names and sit inside the streamed subtree (the parity script strips them first); the artifacts published under a previous login are gone after /login (the board is 87eea43d-b6f0-47fa-a1c4-b10dacb2ff01, P2.24's scan page 8db7e606-3546-498f-b9ff-154db39f0ac5, PR A's review page a4edd962-5678-40c3-8b06-048322339d20, B1's 435d574c-bfa9-4371-afca-2485e813744f, B2's bb8c0ea0-002d-4502-917e-52a6bec1cfbc, PR C's 1ce6f9ab-24e0-48a6-8f00-ea8219a62fde, the progress report 9b8b0a28-91e8-4fca-b08b-985ac612c267).

Commits and PR bodies carry no Co-Authored-By and no Claude attribution. gh pr create may fail; `gh api repos/paris-paraskevas/motorsport/pulls -f title=… -f head=… -f base=main -F body=@file` works and `gh api -X PATCH …/pulls/<n> -F body=@file` updates. New files need the word (a plan's approval counts). After a merge, bring local main forward with `git merge --ff-only origin/main`, never `git reset --hard`. A plan that finds a scope item with no consumer states the conflict in one line and proposes the sharper version as the plan's question (P2.1's rows; P2.6's three conditions).
Browser run recipe (this close's additions in bold): Docker Desktop and the local Supabase up first (`docker info`; `npx supabase start`; REST 200 on 127.0.0.1:54321); a pending migration is applied locally with `npx supabase migration up --local`. The dev server: `PADDOCK_ENV=production npm run dev` in the background with its log in the scratchpad; after any `npm run cf:build`, stop it by the port's PID (`netstat -ano | findstr :3000`, `taskkill //PID <pid> //F`), `rm -rf .next/dev`, start again, and check an anonymous curl of /api/admin/design/pages/<uuid> answers the plain-text "not found" (404). Run cf:build alone, in the foreground, with the dev server stopped by PID and `npx supabase stop` first, never beside vitest or an agent. The sign-in helper lives in the session's scratchpad and is gone with it: rewrite signin-redirect.cjs (a node http server on 127.0.0.1:4599; on GET /go it reads CLERK_SECRET_KEY from .env.local, POSTs https://api.clerk.com/v1/sign_in_tokens with { user_id: 'user_3J40T0N8dWLFMqgMb06DjsgIj3c', expires_in_seconds: 300 } and the bearer key, and answers 302 to http://localhost:3000/sign-in?__clerk_ticket=<token>; logs only "ticket issued"); start it in the background, `page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:4599/go', { waitUntil: 'commit' })`, then `waitForURL(u => u.origin === 'http://localhost:3000' && !u.pathname.startsWith('/sign-in'))`; **`URL` is not defined inside browser_run_code_unsafe: never `new URL(page.url())`, read `page.url().split('?')[0]`**; stop the helper by PID after (`netstat -ano | findstr ":4599 "` → `taskkill //PID <pid> //F`); never grep the dev log without redacting `__clerk_ticket=`. The Playwright browser keeps the Clerk session across dev-server restarts, so check for the toolbar (`[aria-label="Developer toolbar"]`) before signing in again. Silence the support prompt for the tab (`sessionStorage.setItem('paddock:support-prompt', '{"ms":0,"shown":2,"done":true}')`). **Inline `browser_run_code_unsafe` code with absolute screenshot paths (`page.screenshot({ path: 'C:/Dev/Personal/Motorsport/.playwright-mcp/<name>.png' })`, `locator.screenshot(...)` for one pane) needs no script files under .playwright-mcp; the MCP's own console-*.log files live there. Remove the PNGs after embedding them in the review page (a node script base64-inlines them; seven screenshots made a 1.3 MB page).** Set the viewport first (`page.setViewportSize({ width: 1680, height: 1050 })`). The Gallery opens on the region kinds: click `getByRole('button', { name: /^Components$/i }).first()` before `getByLabel('Gallery: <Name>')` (double-click adds to the Body, the working copy only until Save). The Property Editor is `getByRole('region', { name: 'Property Editor', exact: true })`; **its Rules group is open at first, as the common groups are: `getByLabel('Condition type')` is a `<select>` (`selectOption('never')`), then `getByLabel('Condition pages')`, `'Condition part'`, `'Condition value'`; a region's tile is `getByRole('button', { name: 'Static Content: <title>' })`; the centre's tabs are `getByRole('tab', { name: /Component View/i })`, not buttons; the tree is `getByRole('tree', { name: 'Rendering' })` and a struck node's label has `line-through`; Publish is `getByRole('button', { name: 'Publish', exact: true })` with no dialog, status "Published · <time>. Readers see it now.", and the live page rendered the new revision at once**; a component with sources shows in its Source group `getByLabel('Source type')` (None · the sources), then `getByLabel('Series')` and `getByLabel('Season')`; Save is `getByRole('button', { name: 'Save', exact: true })`; the saved document is `GET /api/admin/design/pages/<id>` → newest.document (parsed: no `show` field survives), the preview /preview/<newest.id>, the trace `GET /api/admin/design/debug?rev=<newest.id>&level=app` or `?path=/history/monza&level=app` (the live revision by path). The Data Sources page: `?ws=shared&sc=datasources`, `getByRole('button', { name: 'Open <Source>' })`, the section `getByRole('region', { name: 'Data Source: <Name>' })`, the form `getByRole('form', { name: 'Preview' })` with the parameters as labelled fields and `Load`, the one `role="status"`. The Data workspace has two buttons named Data (the workspace tab and the back button): scope to `page.getByRole('main')`; its `Object Browser` button opens the catalogue. The pages list is `GET /api/admin/design/pages` → `{ pages, deleted }`. The Plug-ins page: `?ws=shared&sc=plugins`, `getByRole('button', { name: 'Open <Name>' })`, the section `getByRole('region', { name: 'Plug-in: <Name>' })`. Playwright's getByRole name matches by substring: `exact: true` when another button's name contains the word. The MCP's `page` may not be the tab you drove last: pick the tab by URL and by the toolbar's presence from `page.context().pages()`. Quick Edit's root `[data-quick-edit]` is an empty div until the pointer finds a region: wait for `state: 'attached'`. A `<img loading="lazy">` in a full-page screenshot renders black. The designer: /admin/designer?ws=builder&page=<id> (Monza 250f3a59-43e6-46b5-b1ef-d1dc7b899fef, its regions Standfirst · The story · Aside · A century of speed · Read more about Imola · What it changed · Elsewhere on Paddock; the Calendar 2bcbe799-39fb-4752-a2d5-50cda49d1761 locally); Shared Components items open with `?sc=<key>`. The local database's default theme is Paper while prod's is Midnight; the local Home has no lead post, so no h1 on /. The local database holds the loader's runs of 2026-09-09 (stale) and the snapshots the laptop's own fetches write.

The renderer: home.changed with ctx.source reads it (changedFromStandings in lib/home-model.ts); RenderContext.source and onSourceRead, RenderHooks.onSourceRead; the Debug trace's render lines carry the source's reads and a line per read. The Property Editor: for a component whose definition declares sources, the Source group draws Location (Catalogue, ours) · Type (aria-label "Source type": None · the sources) · the parameters (Series by the designer's names, filtered to what the source offers; Season the one the loader warms); the tile leads with the pick; series threaded Designer → PageDesigner → ctx. components/designer/DataSourcesEditor.tsx: Shared Components › Data Sources › Data Sources (?ws=shared&sc=datasources; the catalogue's `data` entry renamed to `datasources`; Remote Servers listed "with the first remote source"), and its SourceBrowser in `data` mode as the Data workspace's Object Browser (the button beside "Open the runs"; the loader's work in its two tiers). The routes: GET /api/admin/design/data/sources (Utilization by loadSourceUsage, the rows tier by loadRunsLog, the snapshot tier by getSourceHealth + readSnapshotMeta) and GET /api/admin/design/data/sources/<key>?<params> (the preview, 400 with problems, fifty rows). The Data page's Sources and Runs tables label the loader's keys in the catalogue's words. Every pick names a snapshot the pages keep warm: the season is the loader's (2026), the series those each reader dispatches, the news count 3 (the home wire) or 10 (the News page; MAX_PER_SERIES_AGGREGATE exported from lib/news.ts for the test). A blocking finding is taken in a second commit with the test the reviewer names, and the gates re-run. The decision scan before a slot, a question in one or two sentences with a default and something to look at, then stop; plan mode before code (Bash reads, then EnterPlanMode; a Sonnet plan critic; a critic in plan mode can write only the plan file, so it reports in its final message); tests first, seen failing, then green; gates after the last edit (tsc 0 · lint 0 errors, the two known _encoding warnings · full vitest · node .claude/hooks/test.mjs · cf:build and the wrangler dry run's Total Upload quoted, alarm at 80% of 65536 KiB; last 41610.57 KiB, 63.49%); a browser run with screenshots into a review-page artifact; a fresh-context Sonnet reviewer before merge; merge only on the operator's word (gh pr merge <n> --squash); prod check on /changelog; ledger evidence (status and evidence only; a slot's text changes only by a dated changes line with the word); the trio on every push (the CHANGELOG entry inserted above the previous "## x.y.z" heading, RELEASES prose without paths, package.json bump). Commits carry no Co-Authored-By and no Claude attribution. gh pr create may fail; `gh api repos/paris-paraskevas/motorsport/pulls -f title=… -f head=… -f base=main -F body=@file` works and `gh api -X PATCH …/pulls/<n> -F body=@file` updates. Prod Supabase writes only on "apply <id>"; prod READS through the Management API (POST api.supabase.com/v1/projects/dzelqrtajnauunzmxfic/database/query, the PAT in .supabase-pat, a browser UA). New files need the word (a plan's approval counts). After a merge, bring local main forward with `git merge --ff-only origin/main`, never `git reset --hard`. A plan's decision scan whose third line holds only "the migration on apply <id>" does not stop the slot: the word comes at apply time (P2.0 and P2.1 both ran so); a plan that finds a scope item with no consumer states the conflict in one line and proposes the sharper version as the plan's question (P2.1's rows).
Browser run recipe (this close's additions in bold): Docker Desktop and the local Supabase up first (`docker info`; `npx supabase start`; REST 200 on 127.0.0.1:54321); a pending migration is applied locally with `npx supabase migration up --local`. The dev server: `PADDOCK_ENV=production npm run dev` in the background with its log in the scratchpad; after any `npm run cf:build`, stop it by the port's PID (`netstat -ano | findstr :3000`, `taskkill //PID <pid> //F`), `rm -rf .next/dev`, start again, and check an anonymous curl of /api/admin/design/pages/<uuid> answers the plain-text "not found". The harness kills background tasks when the machine runs low on memory: run cf:build alone, in the foreground, with the dev server stopped by PID and `npx supabase stop` first, never beside vitest or an agent. **The sign-in helper lives in the session's scratchpad and is gone with it: rewrite signin-redirect.cjs at the start of a browser run (a node http server on 127.0.0.1:4599; on GET /go it reads CLERK_SECRET_KEY from .env.local, POSTs https://api.clerk.com/v1/sign_in_tokens with { user_id: 'user_3J40T0N8dWLFMqgMb06DjsgIj3c', expires_in_seconds: 300 } and the bearer key, and answers 302 to http://localhost:3000/sign-in?__clerk_ticket=<token>; logs only "ticket issued"); start it in the background, `page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:4599/go')`, then `waitForURL(u => u.startsWith(base) && !u.includes('/sign-in'))` before navigating on; stop it by PID after; never grep the dev log without redacting `__clerk_ticket=`.** The Playwright browser keeps the Clerk session across dev-server restarts, so check for the toolbar before signing in again. Silence the support prompt for the tab (`sessionStorage.setItem('paddock:support-prompt', '{"ms":0,"shown":2,"done":true}')`). The Playwright MCP reads files from the repo and its .playwright-mcp only: scripts and screenshots go under .playwright-mcp (git-ignored) and are removed after the run; embed screenshots into the review page before removing them; `npm run lint` scans .playwright-mcp, so remove the run scripts before the lint gate. A `filename` script is one bare `async page => { … }` expression: no `export default`, no trailing semicolon. Set the viewport first (`page.setViewportSize({ width: 1680, height: 1050 })`). **The Gallery opens on the region kinds: click `getByRole('button', { name: /^Components$/i }).first()` before `getByLabel('Gallery: <Name>')` (double-click adds to the Body, the working copy only until Save). The Property Editor is `getByRole('region', { name: 'Property Editor', exact: true })`; a component with sources shows in its Source group `getByLabel('Source type')` (None · the sources), then `getByLabel('Series')` and `getByLabel('Season')` once a source is picked; Save is `getByRole('button', { name: 'Save', exact: true })`; the saved document is `GET /api/admin/design/pages/<id>` → newest.document, the preview /preview/<newest.id>, the trace `GET /api/admin/design/debug?rev=<newest.id>&level=app`. The Data Sources page: `?ws=shared&sc=datasources`, `getByRole('button', { name: 'Open <Source>' })`, the section `getByRole('region', { name: 'Data Source: <Name>' })`, the form `getByRole('form', { name: 'Preview' })` with the parameters as labelled fields and `Load`, the one `role="status"`. The Data workspace has two buttons named Data (the workspace tab and the back button): scope to `page.getByRole('main')`; its `Object Browser` button opens the catalogue.** The pages list is `GET /api/admin/design/pages` → `{ pages, deleted }`. The Plug-ins page: `?ws=shared&sc=plugins`, `getByRole('button', { name: 'Open <Name>' })`, the section `getByRole('region', { name: 'Plug-in: <Name>' })`. Playwright's getByRole name matches by substring: `exact: true` when another button's name contains the word. The MCP's `page` may not be the tab you drove last: pick the tab by URL and by the toolbar's presence from `page.context().pages()`. Quick Edit's root `[data-quick-edit]` is an empty div until the pointer finds a region: wait for `state: 'attached'`. `URL` is not defined inside browser_run_code_unsafe. A `<img loading="lazy">` in a full-page screenshot renders black. The designer: /admin/designer?ws=builder&page=<id> (Monza 250f3a59-43e6-46b5-b1ef-d1dc7b899fef; the Calendar 2bcbe799-39fb-4752-a2d5-50cda49d1761 locally); Shared Components items open with `?sc=<key>`. The local database's default theme is Paper while prod's is Midnight; the local Home has no lead post, so no h1 on /. **The local database holds the loader's runs of 2026-09-09 (stale) and the snapshots the laptop's own fetches write, so both tiers show in the Object Browser locally.**
Landmines from session 52: **an artifact the account no longer lists cannot be read by the Artifact tool ("not found"): the Phase 2 screen and the progress board both went; a page that matters is regenerated from the repo's data (docs/plan/phase-2-draft.json, the ledger) by a script in the scratchpad, and its new id recorded.** A Sonnet plan critic changed the model (a region-level field over a new attribute kind) and caught three facts the reads missed (the archive reader throws on the Worker; the runs log holds standings only; a pick could send the Worker upstream): run it before code, every time. **Every catalogue pick must name a snapshot the pages already keep warm (the reviewer's finding): under DATA_SOURCE=db a missing key falls through withSourceSnapshot/withF1LastGood to a live fetch from Cloudflare's egress; when a source gains a parameter, enumerate its values from the pages' own calls, never a range.** vi.spyOn on the real module's namespace for a module the renderers import dynamically (component-render.test.tsx does it for home-model and source-read). The Designer test that stubs fetch must answer by URL and fail the rest: a stub that answers every route with one shape crashes the shell's other loaders. `getByText('Series')` on a section whose form label wraps a select matches only the plain text (a label's textContent includes its options' text); use getAllByText for a word that appears as both. `git switch` to main with an uncommitted file carries it along (IDEAS.md rode to the records branch). A heredoc over about 7 KB fails: long texts through the Write tool, then node.

The MCP's `page` may not be the tab you drove last: pick the tab by URL and by the toolbar's presence from `page.context().pages()`. Quick Edit's root `[data-quick-edit]` is an empty div until the pointer finds a region: wait for `state: 'attached'`; a re-run toggles Quick Edit off again, so read `aria-checked` first. Clearing `paddock:theme` in one page resets every open tab of the origin. Save and Run reuses the one named running tab and no longer opens the preview: open /preview/<newest.id> yourself. The Property Editor's Header and Footer group is closed by default; the Appearance group too: click the group's header button before reading its rows. A drag with `page.mouse` needs the source tile scrolled into view first. `URL` is not defined inside browser_run_code_unsafe. A `<img loading="lazy">` in a full-page screenshot renders black. The designer: /admin/designer?ws=builder&page=<id> (Monza 250f3a59-43e6-46b5-b1ef-d1dc7b899fef; the Calendar 2bcbe799-39fb-4752-a2d5-50cda49d1761 locally); Shared Components items open with `?sc=<key>`. The local database's default theme is Paper while prod's is Midnight; the local Home has no lead post, so no h1 on /.
Landmines from session 51 (all three closes): a vi.mock factory is bypassed by concurrent dynamic imports of one module from one importer (vitest 4.1.6): spy on the real module's namespace. Never take Escape at the document in the capture phase on a running page: a panel takes Escape on its own element. A Bash heredoc over about 7 KB fails with "unexpected EOF while looking for matching `''" and NOTHING in that command runs (it bit three times today): long texts and scripts go through the Write tool, then `node script.cjs`; byte-level splices keep CRLF files intact; `Buffer.indexOf` wants a utf8 Buffer for an anchor with an em dash; `sed -i` on a CRLF file rewrites it as LF. A node script that inserts an import "after the last `import` line" must skip multi-line import blocks and split on the file's own newline (CRLF or LF), else the line lands inside a block. Composed versus row is `page.kind !== 'row'`, never `served === 'rows'`. A header or footer inside article/aside/main/nav/section is no landmark. The react-hooks lint refuses a synchronous setState inside an effect. A variable declared inside `if (r.kind === 'component') {…}` is not visible to code after the block: hoist what later groups read (the capability flags). **A page's document carries every attribute of its components from its next save (componentDefaults fills them all), and the parser drops a component region whose settings hold a key its definition lacks (R2b: "an unknown key is a problem the writer refuses"): so a shared-object guard on an attribute must count the KEY's presence, and an added attribute is permanent once used (the Inbox question). A 60-second per-isolate memo is fine for readers and wrong for a write path's guard: read the row fresh there (the P2.0 B reviewer's finding).**
Agenda for session 52: 1) P2.1 Data Sources: the decision scan, plan mode with a Sonnet plan critic, tests first, build, gates, browser run, review page, reviewer, PR, the word, merge, the migration on "apply <id>" with the operator present. 2) If time: P2.6 the Conditions vocabulary (no word needed; a drawing of the Rules group against the seven show rules is on the Phase 2 screen). 3) Close: HANDOFF, SCHEDULE, IDEAS triage, ledger evidence, one records PR; regenerate the board.

Stop the dev server and the redirect server by their PIDs (netstat :3000, :4599): stopping the harness's background task kills only the wrapper shell, the next dev child keeps port 3000, a second start moves to 3001, and clearing .next/dev under the live server yields 500 on every route. A dev server whose admin design APIs answer Next's HTML 404 page is stale although the routes sit in its manifest (seen once on 2026-09-15, cause not found): an anonymous curl of /api/admin/design/pages must answer the route's own plain-text "not found"; else restart on a cleared .next/dev. The tab Save and Run opens has a small viewport, so the Developer Toolbar is hidden until setViewportSize(1440, 900). The MCP browser can keep its frames paused for a whole run (requestAnimationFrame never fires while visibilityState reads visible and bringToFront changes nothing): prove next-frame focus and caret by stubbing rAF with a timer for the check, and say so. Playwright refuses a plain click on an aria-disabled element ("element is not enabled"), itself evidence; { force: true } proves the handlers are off. The tool echoes the final URL, spent ticket included, into the transcript even through the redirect; mint per run, never paste.

Each of the three is asked when its turn comes, not before; the documentRefs gap (Header Text and Footer Text shortcuts not projected into the refs) is offered as a quick slot before Phase 2 if the operator says so. 5) Once all of Phase 1 is done: the Phase 2 screen, one page with the draft's 25 slots (P2.0 the component definition model first, P2.1 Data Sources, P2.2 the data region with views, then the components) and a decision scan, for the word "Phase 2 go" or a changed list; a dated changes line records it. 6) Close: HANDOFF, SCHEDULE, IDEAS triage, ledger evidence, one records PR; regenerate the board.

Earlier in the day: stopping a background `npm run dev` through the harness kills only the wrapper shell and the next dev child keeps port 3000; stop it by the port's PID first, or the next start moves to 3001 and clearing .next/dev under the live one yields 500 everywhere. A stale .next/dev can answer the DYNAMIC API routes (pages/[id], lists/[key]) with Next's HTML 404 page while static routes work and the manifest lists them: check with an anonymous curl of /api/admin/design/pages/<uuid> (must be the route's plain-text "not found"), else restart on a cleared cache. The lists' and the frames' 60-second memos are reset by the API routes, but under next dev routes and pages compile apart, so the layout keeps the old list for up to a minute locally (the anonymous home was clean within ninety seconds); on the Worker the isolate that wrote sees it at once. A Bash heredoc of ~8 KB or more fails before running ("unexpected EOF while looking for matching quote"): write long node scripts to the scratchpad with the Write tool and run the file; several working-copy files are CRLF, so a node script that edits them must normalise line endings and write them back (the scratchpad's edit-*.cjs pattern). `URL` is not defined inside browser_run_code_unsafe; read a query parameter with a regex on page.url(). The Create page dialog draws its hint in small caps through CSS, so a regex on innerText misses the words; read the element or the screenshot. Playwright refuses a plain click on an aria-disabled element ("element is not enabled"), itself evidence. The tab Save and Run opens has a small viewport; call setViewportSize before reading the Developer Toolbar. The MCP browser can keep its frames paused for a whole run (requestAnimationFrame never fires while visibilityState reads visible): prove next-frame focus by stubbing rAF with a timer and say so. The Appearance document's 60-second memo means a saved preset (or face) reaches a page after up to a minute on any isolate but the one that served the save, and the dev server compiles routes and pages apart so it waits the minute locally too. A `<label htmlFor>` pointing at a button names the button. The Property Pane keeps a group's fold by title across selections and the page's Appearance group starts folded. A click on the site's support prompt counts as a click outside a region and exits Quick Edit, as APEX's does. A named target is found among the tabs familiar with the source (its opener and that opener's children): two running tabs opened by hand each keep a developer tab of their own. The one-shot pagesFilter: a group chosen through Create › Page Group… filters the pages list for that return alone. The back arrow must call onBack() bare, since its argument is a filter, not the click event. The reviewer's gaps are taken in a second commit and the gates re-run after it. P1.11's: the Property Pane keys its rows by label and keeps a group's fold across a selection change, so any stateful control inside a row must be keyed by the region (key={r.id}, as the Template Options button and now the text picker are) or its state survives into the next region; the tree selects on Enter without a mousedown, so an outside-click guard never fires for a keyboard reselection. documentRefs scans Static Content's text for shortcuts, not Header Text or Footer Text. A commented-out region stays in the refs on purpose (the write path stores the document as drawn), so its rows stay undeletable while it is on the page.
```

### Landmines learned this morning
- A Bash command over roughly 8 KB fails in this harness with "unexpected EOF while looking for matching quote" before anything runs; write long texts (release entries, PR bodies, scripts) with the Write tool and reference the file.
- A Bash command containing a literal control character (a newline inside a JS string) is refused outright ("control characters that would be hidden in the approval dialog"); put the script in a file.
- `pg_stat_user_tables.n_live_tup` is an estimate and reads 0 for tables never analysed; count with `select count(*)` (the Data tab does).
- `.next/dev/types/routes.d.ts` can be left mid-write by a running `next dev` and fail `tsc` with syntax errors; stop the server (by PID from the port) and clear `.next/dev/types` and `.next/types`.
- The admin root's `<html>` has no `suppressHydrationWarning`, so an operator whose stored console mode differs from the server's default sees a hydration warning (the "1 Issue" badge in development). Pre-existing, one attribute to fix, not done.

### Landmines learned tonight
- Turbopack refuses a `node_modules` junction or symlink that resolves outside the project root; a second worktree needs its own `npm ci`. Its `.next` is its own, so a build there never clobbers the main tree's `next dev`.
- A **cross-request memo in a loader that feeds a cached page is unsafe on publish**: the process that regenerates the page may hold the stale copy and re-cache it until the next publish. Per-request `cache()` for anything a publish changes. The 60 s memos that remain (`loadPageFrame`, the application definition, the lists, the shortcuts) carry the same hazard in principle for one regeneration after a save; accepted on 2026-09-08 and left as is.
- Two PRs' files in one working tree: commit the already-merged one as a duplicate, then `git rebase --autostash origin/main`; git drops the duplicate ("skipped previously applied commit") and the other PR's files come back on top of main, nothing lost.
- `next dev` under CPU pressure (a concurrent `next build`) can register a route late and hand its requests to `[...catchall]` (an HTML 404 where a route's own answer is `text/plain`); `touch` the route file.
- `gh pr merge --delete-branch` run from a secondary worktree merges, then fails to check out `main` ("already used by worktree") and leaves the remote branch in place.

### Landmines learned tonight
- A `! grep … | head` guard inside an `&&` chain takes head's exit status, so the chain stopped before `git add`, and `git push` then pushed the branch at main's commit ("No commits between main and …" from `gh pr create`). Guard with `if grep -q …; then exit 1; fi`, and grep the diff of the new entries, not the whole changelog (old entries carry the three-letter placeholder word from 2026).
- A prod rehearsal that exercises a function must count in a **separate statement**: a statement's sub-selects share one snapshot and do not see what volatile functions inserted in its CTEs (a first attempt counted `revisions 0` beside `publish_stamped true`).
- Full-page screenshots of the console need its `fixed inset-0` root released to `relative` and every scroll pane opened (`shot-full.mjs` in the session scratchpad); Playwright's `fullPage` alone returns the viewport.
- `/changelog` states the version as `Currently running v 1.0.58` (capital C, a `v` before the number); a case-sensitive poll for "currently running" never matches.
- `date -u` is the clock to quote: the earlier notes estimated Z times from the local clock (UTC+2) and were two hours late.

### Landmines learned this session
- Stopping `next dev` right before `cf:build` can leave `.next/dev/types/routes.d.ts` truncated; the build's type step reads it (`';' expected`) and removing one file leaves `validator.ts` importing it. Clear `.next/dev/types` whole. A production build under a running dev server also 404s dynamic routes on the next dev start until `.next/dev` is fresh.
- GitHub **closes** a stacked PR when its base branch is deleted by the squash merge, and a closed PR's base cannot be changed: rebase onto main, force-push with lease, open a new PR (#929 → #930).
- `node -e … "$sql"` cannot take an argument that begins with `--`: the migration's leading comment dashes were read as a node option and nothing was sent. Pass SQL through the environment (`process.env.SQL`).
- Playwright is not in the repo; the scratchpad has its own `node_modules/playwright` (1.63, chromium-headless-shell 1243). Static markup carries no `<select>` selection: mark the chosen option before reading `innerHTML` for a render probe.
- Tailwind v4 utilities from `@theme inline` inline their values: overriding `--font-sans` reaches the body rule only. Role variables inside the stacks are the single point that reaches every utility.
- `gh pr merge` right after a push to the same branch can fail with "Base branch was modified. Review and try the merge again" while GitHub recomputes mergeability; wait a few seconds and retry, nothing is wrong.
- A placeholder left in a committed file is found by grepping for every placeholder name before the commit, not most of them: `APPLY_LINE` reached PR #932 and was fixed by a second commit before the merge.

### Decisions (operator, 2026-09-08 midday)
- **Rows at render are fine** for what changes between deploys, with the code as the fallback and a per-isolate memo ("is it clever to have queriable pages?" answered: structure stays code, values are rows). · **Custom themes now**, as a shipped base plus nine colours; the shipped six keep their colours in CSS. · **Authorization schemes**: name and message only; add, remove and enforcement with Phase 3. · **Settings**: the blog band's visibility stays the composer's, the news source map stays code; "default series for new visitors" became the home lead and featured series (the 2026-09-04 decision as rows). · A minute for a save to show everywhere is acceptable.

### Findings worth carrying
1. **Next integrates `window.history.replaceState`** (its linking guide), so a browser-only chunk keeps state in the URL without a server round trip, and `replaceState` keeps the console's back arrow one step.
2. **A partial unique index can trip inside one UPDATE** that clears one row's flag and sets another's; the safe shape is clear-then-set inside a plpgsql function, one transaction (`design_set_default_theme`).
3. **`revalidatePath('/series/[slug]/weekend/[round]', 'page')` from a Route Handler only marks pages** for a fresh render on their next visit; nothing renders at once (Next's docs). The weekend pages' five-minute cache therefore does not delay a switch.
4. **Testing Library under jsdom tests a client component's behaviour** once its heavy children are mocked and `fetch` is stubbed (`QualifyingDecoder.test.tsx`, `Designer.test.tsx`); the render probe uses the same to reach states a static render cannot (a changed field, a 409 banner).
5. **`'fail' in g` over an object-literal union leaves `g.fail` possibly undefined**; type the gate as a discriminated union (`themes/[key]/route.ts`).
6. **Stacked PRs**: build the second on the first's branch, `git rebase main` after the first squash-merges (git drops the identical patch), `git push --force-with-lease`, check `gh pr view --json files`, then merge.
7. **A custom theme's rule has the same specificity as a shipped theme's** (`:root[attr]`); it wins by coming later, the `<style>` in body after the stylesheet. Its base keeps supplying `color-scheme`, the dark family and the light themes' per-element tint rule.
8. **`eslint-disable` for `react-hooks/exhaustive-deps` is never the fix**: inline the reads or move the helper to module scope (done twice, both times before the commit).
9. **Every design loader has the same four failure paths** (unconfigured, error, empty, unusable row) and the same test file shape; a new one takes twenty minutes when copied from `lib/design/text.ts`.

### Next, flat
1. Shortcuts editor (small), then Assets (the R2 upload path; caption, credit, licence), each with the recipe.
2. **Phase 3, App Builder**: `page` + `page_revision` with the refs projection, the Layout schematic, region renderers, the catch-all route with noindex until indexable, redirect rows, the dynamic-action interpreter, the route-collision test; authz enforcement (one evaluator; the nav needs a client-side one because layouts render at ISR time); Social/Studio gating; add/remove schemes.
3. Phase 0 follow-up: multi-class standings (`manufacturer` kind, forward migration, morning apply).
4. Housekeeping: `supabase/README.md` is stale about migrations; the memo option; the release header; the six branch deletions.

### Findings worth carrying (morning)
1. **Stop `wrangler dev` by its `npx` root PID with `/T`.** Killing the port's PID leaves workerd and esbuild alive holding `.open-next`, and the next `cf:build` dies with `EPERM … rm .open-next`. Two such trees were found and ended this morning.
2. **Screenshots of admin UI without a session**: render the component with `renderToStaticMarkup` under vitest (mock `next/navigation` and `@clerk/nextjs`), wrap the markup in the build's CSS (`.next/static/chunks/*.css`), serve the folder with `python -m http.server` (Playwright blocks `file://`), screenshot at 1440×900, publish as an artifact. Worked first time for #918.
3. **`react-hooks/set-state-in-effect`** fires on syncing state from a prop in an effect; the fix is React's own pattern (adjust during render with a `seen` state), never a disable.
4. The Management API `database/query` endpoint runs a `begin … rollback` script as a real rehearsal: every statement executes against prod and nothing persists. Used before all three applies.
